# Consciousness Phase 02 — formal / neural / cognitive / phenomenal role audit v0.1

## 0. Status

```text
Consciousness audit: IN PROGRESS
Phase 00 dependency / inheritance / source authority: COMPLETE
Phase 01 equation / symbol / object inventory: COMPLETE
Phase 02 formal / neural / cognitive / phenomenal role audit: COMPLETE
Phase 03 preload / observer boundary / self-reference / circularity audit: NOT STARTED
Upstream VED equation audit / core revision: CLOSED / CLOSED
Upstream Bio-IFGT audit / core revision: CLOSED / CLOSED
No source/equation/notation revised; no claim disposition assigned
```

本記録はsourceが担わせる役割の分類であり、役割移行の成立を認定しない。神経・認知機構の成立、現象的説明の成否、循環性、preloadの問題性、実証適合・証拠十分性、理論の妥当性は判定しない。R0–R3と各ledger IDは監査用ラベルであり、理論の新notationではない。2026-09-09の作業開始時repository現物を基準とする。

## 1. Authority

[Phase 00](00_dependency_inheritance_and_authority_record_v0.1_ja.md)、[Phase 01](01_equation_symbol_object_inventory_v0.1_ja.md)、[README](README_ja.md)をinventory authorityとし、下表の実sourceを照合した。Phase 01の178出現ID・52式形familyを変更しない。上流closureは本branchのrole transitionへ継承しない。

[VED final audit](../ved_equation_audit/06_final_audit_record_v1.0_ja.md)、[Bio final audit](../bio_ifgt_audit/07_final_audit_record_v1.0_ja.md)、[Bio final recheck](../bio_ifgt_audit/implementation_final_consistency_recheck_v1.1_ja.md)は上流scopeのauthorityであり、Consciousnessのphenomenal同定を承認するauthorityではない。

S00–S20はPhase 00のsection locator。表でS番号のみを記す場合、同番号EN/JAの当該小見出し・語句を指す。式は出現行を個別指定する。現在のEN/JA modular TeX、working Markdown、図PDF、配布PDFを統合・修正しない。source自身の説明層L1–L3と監査R0–R3は別体系。

## 2. Role taxonomy

| Role | 範囲 | 付与条件 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| R0 | definition / parameter / difference / ratio / field / balance / constitutive / condition / schematic map | 数式・定義・形式条件としてのsource使用 |
| R1 | neural / biological carrier, feedback, pathway, bodily substrate | neural/brain/body等の明示を必要とする。fastという名前だけでは付与しない |
| R2 | prediction / memory / log reference / attention / report / self-model / action / introspection | 機能としてのsource記述 |
| R3 | conscious state / opening / qualia / phenomenal surface / experienced ego/time | 経験・意識regimeへのsourceの明示的接続 |

Guards: formal definition ≠ neural mechanism; neural interpretation ≠ cognitive function; cognitive function ≠ phenomenal experience; functional sufficiency ≠ phenomenal identity; formal correspondence ≠ mechanistic derivation; analogy ≠ carrier identity; necessary condition ≠ sufficient condition; source says “is” ≠ audit establishes identity。

「なし／未指定」は当該式・文で個別役割が指定されないという所在記録であり、機構不在や証拠不足の判定ではない。「R3接続」はsourceのregime labelも含み、phenomenal identityの成立認定ではない。HIGH等は同一語の多役割を後続で分解する必要度で、claim強度・妥当性の採点ではない。

## 3. Equation-role audit

178出現（EN 73 / JA 105）を各一行、52式形familyをF01–F52として表示する。反復式でも役割は出現章ごとに記録した。exact formはPhase 01の同一CON-EQに保持され、本phaseで数式を再定義しない。R1をS14の総論から各係数へ逆輸入しない。

Phase 01のTIME-08/FIG-SYM-051等の説明表には、別式を指す短縮EQ参照がある。本表は説明表の短縮番号でなく、178個のexact-form entryと現物の式を突き合わせた。preparation→reportはCON-EQ-048/130、asymmetric reportは049/131、qualia→residueは051/053/133/135である。旧記録は変更しない。

## 9. Log-referencing loop roles

S09のenumerateをそのまま六段階として分類する。gate passageとslow sedimentationはsource step2内で連続しており、I更新とPhi更新がstep3/4に分かれる。依頼の機能リストとの違いを隠して新しい六段階に組み替えない。

| LOOP-STEP-003 | sedimented logs alter I | field update description | no cell-level update given | history configuration changes | no independent phenomenal field asserted here |
| LOOP-STEP-004 | I configuration alters Phi | field/potential relation | no molecular potential given | meaning landscape changes | appearance bias follows later |
| LOOP-STEP-005 | Phi gradient biases referencing and fast processing | effective direction | no explicit neural controller | future selection/prioritization | source links what appears, not all bias conscious |
| LOOP-STEP-006 | new fast activity unfolds under bias | recurrence | generic neural implementation elsewhere | feedback to candidate activity | whole-loop conscious regime claim distinct |

| Whole-loop role | Source wording / distinction |
| --- | --- |
| feedback architecture | S09 reciprocal fast/slow, not one-way transfer |
| self-reference | S04/S09 own sedimented logs reference and update the same ongoing process |
| cognitive persistence | S09 logs retain bias while update continues |
| conscious state | S09 Through this loop, the conscious state is sustained / nonclosure loop |
| mechanism-language | S09 maintains/drives/biases assigns causal-style functional and phenomenal wording; no circularity judgment |

### Literal conditional / mechanism scan coverage

EN/JA current section42ファイル全てを対象とした再検索。条件候補にはnecessary/sufficient/condition/required/requires/only if/if/when/must/unless、および必要/十分/条件/場合/とき/なら/なければ等を含める。headings・質問・読書指示・研究要求を混ぜて必要条件数を水増ししない。以下は全candidate paragraphのlocatorと語句を保持する補助索引であり、NS-ROLE family表が論理方向のclassification authority。節番号と行を省略しない。

| Occurrence | Source | Literal context class | Source paragraph (unmodified wording; whitespace flattened) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| NS-OCC-001 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):4 | question-containing context; do not infer assertion | ``` Discussions of consciousness typically begin with questions such as: where is it generated, which circuits are responsible, and which representational format does it correspond to? This paper changes the framing of these questions. Consciousness is not treated here as something produced somewhere; it is modeled as a state that opens when processes at different time scales become mutually referenceable while maintaining a certain delay and coupling. ``` |
| NS-OCC-002 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):6 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` In this view, consciousness is not identical to the self, to thought, or to judgment. It is the condition under which fast predictive processes become referenceable to the layers of slow memory, reporting, and sedimentation. Accordingly, the aim of this paper is not to provide a final definition of consciousness, but to establish a structural position from which consciousness can be rigorously discussed. ``` |
| NS-OCC-003 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):19 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` More precisely, consciousness is not modeled here as a generated object. It is treated as a state (a log-referencing state) that opens when a specific speed difference, coupling viscosity, internal delay, and meaning gradient are established between fast predictive processes and slow sedimentary memory processes. This difference is not merely definitional; it concerns the structure of the question itself — what should be asked about consciousness. ``` |
| NS-OCC-004 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):23 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` This paper does not reduce consciousness to a single physical quantity, information measure, or integration index. Consciousness is not reducible to any one of: speed difference $\Delta v$, coupling viscosity $\mu$, internal delay $\tau$, effective torque-like quantity $\mathcal{T}_{\Phi}$, or meaning gradient $\nabla\Phi$. Consciousness is a temporal non-closure regime that obtains when these variables enter a certain relation. ``` |
| NS-OCC-005 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):29 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` This paper holds that consciousness does not localize to a single site. This is not a negation of neuroscience. It is entirely possible that specific brain regions, circuits, oscillations, or activity patterns correlate with conscious states. In the framework of this paper, these are interpreted as local conditions that participate in the log-referencing loop. ``` |
| NS-OCC-006 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):31 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Neural correlates of consciousness are not consciousness itself. They are part of the local substrate through which consciousness opens. The shift from "finding the seat of consciousness" to "investigating the conditions under which consciousness opens" is the stance this paper takes toward neuroscience. ``` |
| NS-OCC-007 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):39 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` At the same time, the IFGT variables \(I\), \(J_I\), \(\Phi\), and the local log-referencing flow \(J_{\log}\) are used not as direct identifications with the general IFGT field, but as projected effective coordinates within the biological-cognitive observation window. This methodological stance, projected uses, and the structural definition of the projection operation are developed in §6. The role of the present declaration is to make clear in advance, when the reader encounters projection or field vocabulary from §2 onward, that these terms are restricted to the structural level. ``` |
| NS-OCC-008 | [S02 EN](../../consciousness/sections/intuitive_picture.tex):8 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` This boundary is not a static location. It is a dynamic relation that obtains only when the speed difference between the fast and slow processes is maintained while their coupling does not break. When the speed difference vanishes, the boundary disappears. When the speed difference is too large, referencing ruptures.  ``` |
| NS-OCC-009 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):7 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Before entering this section, one point must be fixed. ``` |
| NS-OCC-010 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):15 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` The second is slow processing, which retains successful response conditions for the next generation or next state. It is sedimentary and stable; structures once inscribed do not readily dissolve. ``` |
| NS-OCC-011 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):23 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Accordingly, when this paper introduces SSO as the minimal temporal structure for consciousness, it adopts the proposed fast/slow interpretation as its own downstream premise. The relation to Bio-IFGT remains a structural motivation rather than a result established by the Bio simulation. ``` |
| NS-OCC-012 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):67 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` The minimal condition for SSO is that the two layers are not running at the same speed. ``` |
| NS-OCC-013 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):77 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` It is, rather, the condition for SSO to obtain. ``` |
| NS-OCC-014 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):79 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If $\Delta v = 0$, the fast and slow layers are fully synchronized, and no boundary arises between them. ``` |
| NS-OCC-015 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):83 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Therefore, at least the following condition is necessary for a conscious state to obtain: ``` |
| NS-OCC-016 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):91 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If the speed difference is too large, the correspondence between the layers is lost and log referencing fragments. ``` |
| NS-OCC-017 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):93 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` What SSO requires is a finite speed difference that neither vanishes nor ruptures. ``` |
| NS-OCC-018 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):127 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` The interaction between the fast and slow layers requires a plasticity gate. ``` |
| NS-OCC-019 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):133 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If all activity of the fast layer were written into the slow layer, the system would become excessively rigid. ``` |
| NS-OCC-020 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):135 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Conversely, if fast-layer activity never reached the slow layer, experience would not sediment and log referencing would not obtain. ``` |
| NS-OCC-021 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):139 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` It is a temporal passage condition involving speed difference, delay, and viscosity. ``` |
| NS-OCC-022 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):141 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Under this passage condition, only a portion of fast processing sediments into the slow layer and becomes a re-referenceable log. ``` |
| NS-OCC-023 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):162 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If $\tau$ is too small, processing flows away as immediate reaction and rarely becomes a referenceable log. ``` |
| NS-OCC-024 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):164 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If $\tau$ is too large, the correspondence between current processing and log sedimentation breaks down and the conscious state fragments. ``` |
| NS-OCC-025 | [S04 EN](../../consciousness/sections/sso.tex):179 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` But if closure is complete, the speed difference vanishes, flow stops, and the boundary of consciousness is lost. ``` |

| NS-OCC-138 | [S11 EN](../../consciousness/sections/qualia_boundary.tex):181 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` In the moments when humans feel "I am conscious right now," some processing is in the process of sedimenting, while at the same time it has not yet fully become a log. ``` |

| NS-OCC-140 | [S11 EN](../../consciousness/sections/qualia_boundary.tex):212 | question-containing context; do not infer assertion | ``` Not "where are qualia?" but "under what conditions is processing experienced as a boundary just before being logged?" ``` |

| NS-OCC-142 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):8 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` It is a structural description: when the fast layer, slow layer, plasticity gate, meaning gradient, and internal delay enter a certain relation, a log-referenceable passage is temporarily opened. ``` |
| NS-OCC-143 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):31 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Consciousness is not treated here as the product of processing; it is the state that opens when the relation between processing and logs enters a certain temporal condition. ``` |
| NS-OCC-144 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):42 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` For this relation to obtain, at least the following conditions are required: ``` |

| NS-OCC-147 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):69 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If the gate is completely closed, processing does not reach the slow layer. Even though processing runs, log referencing does not open. ``` |

| NS-OCC-152 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):110 | question-containing context; do not infer assertion | ``` In the opening model, the question changes to: under what conditions does the boundary of log referencing appear as a live foreground? ``` |
| NS-OCC-153 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):129 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` When the opening closes, the conscious state does not persist. ``` |

| NS-OCC-158 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):177 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` What consciousness requires is the middle. ``` |
| NS-OCC-159 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):185 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` It treats consciousness as the opening that obtains when non-closure has stabilized. ``` |
| NS-OCC-160 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):198 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` If consciousness is a temporary opening, the self is the terrain formed after that opening has repeatedly passed through. ``` |
| NS-OCC-161 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):213 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` \begin{itemize} \item Consciousness is not a product. \item Consciousness is the state in which referenceability has opened between fast processing and slow logs. \item The plasticity gate is not a device that produces consciousness but a boundary condition regulating log-referencing opening. \item Qualia are the live foreground appearing at the opening boundary. \item What is stored is not consciousness but the logs updated while consciousness was open. \item What can be copied is logs and structure, not consciousness itself. \item Consciousness opens within the range of temporal non-closure, not at complete closure or complete opening. \item The self is not the cause of consciousness but the slow-layer terrain formed through the repetition of opening. \end{itemize} ``` |
| NS-OCC-162 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):228 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Consciousness obtains when log referencing opens within temporal non-closure.  ``` |
| NS-OCC-163 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):15 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Measurement requires stabilization. ``` |

| NS-OCC-165 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):23 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Consciousness is a temporal non-closure regime that opens when speed difference, coupling viscosity, internal delay, and meaning gradient enter a certain range. ``` |
| NS-OCC-166 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):130 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Measurement is the necessary transformation for handling live boundary states. ``` |
| NS-OCC-167 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):176 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` What should be measured is under what conditions qualia appear, under what conditions they sediment, and what residue they leave. ``` |
| NS-OCC-168 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):215 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` \begin{itemize} \item Qualia are the live boundary state appearing at the sedimentation frontier. \item Measurement sediments that boundary state into the slow layer. \item What remains after measurement is sedimented residue, not qualia themselves. \item As measurement precision increases, residue becomes more definite, but live qualia are closed. \item Introspection is also internal measurement and logs qualia. \item Measurement is not error but transformation for handling boundary states. \item What should be experimentally treated is not qualia themselves but the conditions and residue of qualia sedimentation. \end{itemize} ``` |
| NS-OCC-169 | [S14 EN](../../consciousness/sections/non_localization.tex):12 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` In the position of this paper, consciousness is not a location but a temporal regime that opens when speed difference, coupling viscosity, internal delay, meaning gradient, and log referencing enter a certain relation. ``` |
| NS-OCC-170 | [S14 EN](../../consciousness/sections/non_localization.tex):50 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` Consciousness is the regime that opens when local substrates temporally couple and enter a log-referenceable relation. ``` |

| NS-OCC-186 | [S14 EN](../../consciousness/sections/non_localization.tex):166 | question-containing context; do not infer assertion | ``` \begin{itemize} \item Consciousness has neural substrates. \item But the conscious state itself does not localize to a single site. \item What localization captures is not live consciousness but local substrates or sedimented residue. \item Consciousness is the temporal relation formed by speed difference, coupling viscosity, internal delay, meaning gradient, and plasticity gate. \item Neural correlates are important, but they should be interpreted not as consciousness itself but as part of the log-referencing loop. \item Non-local does not mean diffuse; it means not reducible to a single site. \item The appropriate question is not "where is consciousness?" but "under what conditions does the conscious state open?" \end{itemize} ``` |

| NS-OCC-203 | [S15 EN](../../consciousness/sections/qualitative_predictions.tex):202 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` \begin{enumerate} \item Correspondence between the time-scale difference of neural activity and the stability of subjective report. \item Changes in $\tau$ through attentional manipulation. \item Increase in referencing bias for semantically loaded stimuli. \item Transformation of qualia-like reports by strengthened measurement and report demands. \item Changes in speed difference, viscosity, and delay window in sleep, dreaming, anesthesia, and dissociative states. \item Asymmetry of log-referencing pathways in linguistically non-reportable processing. \item Clarification of residue and transformation of live qualia reports through measurement demand intensity. \item Changes in conscious report due to differences in temporal coupling conditions for identical local activity. \item Phase-diagram classification of conscious states on axes of speed difference and coupling viscosity. \end{enumerate} ``` |

| NS-OCC-242 | [S18 EN](../../consciousness/sections/discussion.tex):230 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` \begin{itemize} \item This paper treats consciousness not as a product but as the opening of log referencing. \item It has points of contact with Global Workspace, IIT, Higher-Order, and Predictive Processing discussions, but is not identical to them. \item This paper does not reduce consciousness to a single site, single quantity, or single representation. \item It does not deny neuroscience; it reinterprets neural correlates as local conditions of the log-referencing loop. \item IFGT and VED are used not as theories that fully explain consciousness but as coordinate systems for positioning it. \item The claim of this paper is shifting the question in consciousness research from "generation" to "opening conditions." \item Unresolved tasks remain, but this corresponds not only to the incompleteness of the theory but also to the non-closure of consciousness as its object. \end{itemize} ``` |

| NS-OCC-257 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):4 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` 意識をめぐる議論はしばしば、「どこで生成されるのか」「どの回路が担うのか」「どの表象形式に対応するのか」という問いから始まる。本稿は、この問いの置き方自体を変更する。意識は本稿ではどこかで作られる対象として扱われず、異なる時間スケールの処理が、一定の遅延と結合を保ったまま相互参照可能になるときに開かれる状態としてモデル化される。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-258 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):6 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` この見方では、意識は自己、思考、判断そのものではない。意識は、高速な予測過程が低速な記憶・報告・沈殿の層へ参照可能になる条件である。したがって、本稿の目的は意識の最終定義を与えることではなく、意識を論じるための構造的位置を明確にすることである。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-259 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):18 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` より正確には、本稿では意識を生成された対象としてモデル化しない。意識は、高速な予測過程と低速な沈殿的記憶過程のあいだに、特定の速度差・結合粘性・内部遅延・意味勾配が成立したときに開かれる状態（ログ参照状態）として扱われる。この違いは定義上の差異ではなく、意識について何を問うべきかという問いの構造そのものに関わる。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-260 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):22 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` 本稿は、意識を単一の物理量・情報量・統合度に帰着させない。意識は、速度差 $\Delta v$、結合粘性 $\mu$、内部遅延 $\tau$、有効トルク様量 $\mathcal{T}_{\Phi}$、意味勾配 $\nabla\Phi$ のいずれか一つに還元されるものではない。意識は、それらの変数が一定の関係に入ったときに成立する時間的非閉包レジームである。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-261 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):28 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` 本稿は、意識が単一部位に局在しないと主張する。しかしこれは神経科学の否定ではない。ある脳部位・回路・振動・活動パターンが意識状態と相関することは十分にありうる。本稿の立場では、それらはログ参照ループに参加する局所的条件として解釈される。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-262 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):30 | source conditional/condition context; NS family classification above | ``` 神経相関（neural correlates of consciousness）は意識そのものではない。それは、意識が開かれるための局所基盤の一部である。「意識の座を探す」問いから「意識が開かれる条件を調べる」問いへの移行が、本稿の神経科学に対するスタンスである。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-263 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):34 | method/reading/research context; not automatically consciousness criterion | ``` 本稿は projection という語彙を用いる。読者がこの語に最初に接するときに、あらかじめ宣言しておくべき点がある。 ``` |
| NS-OCC-264 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):38 | method/reading/research context; not automatically consciousness criterion | ``` 同時に、本稿における IFGT 変数 $I$、$J_I$、$\Phi$、および局所的 log-referencing 流 $J_{\log}$ は、一般 IFGT 場との直接的同定としてではなく、生物-認知観測窓における投影された有効座標として用いられる。この方法論的立場、すなわち投影的使用と、projection 操作の構造論的定義は、§6 で詳述される。本宣言の役割は、読者が §2 以降で projection や場の語彙に出会うときに、これらが構造論的水準に限定されていることをあらかじめ確認しておくことにある。 ``` |

mechanism候補索引は§24の34 familyを補う所在検索で、includes/headings/否定・比喩をmechanismの成立へ読み替えない。

| Occurrence | Source | Source wording/context |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ML-OCC-001 | [S00 EN](../../consciousness/sections/abstract.tex):1 | ``` This paper formulates consciousness not as an internally generated object, but as a state that opens temporarily between processes operating at different time scales. The central claim is that consciousness, in this descriptive layer, is not treated as a specific brain region, representation, or output, but as a log-referenceable relation established between fast predictive processes and slow sedimentary memory processes. ``` |
| ML-OCC-002 | [S00 EN](../../consciousness/sections/abstract.tex):3 | ``` To describe this relation, we introduce internal delay or buffering as $\tau$, an effective torque-like quantity as $\mathcal{T}$, and information potential as $\Phi$. Speed difference is written $\Delta v = v_f - v_s$ and coupling viscosity as $\mu$. Libet-type experiments, split-brain experiments, qualia, measurement, and non-localization are all reinterpreted from the perspective of inter-scale transformation and incomplete closure. ``` |
| ML-OCC-003 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):4 | ``` Discussions of consciousness typically begin with questions such as: where is it generated, which circuits are responsible, and which representational format does it correspond to? This paper changes the framing of these questions. Consciousness is not treated here as something produced somewhere; it is modeled as a state that opens when processes at different time scales become mutually referenceable while maintaining a certain delay and coupling. ``` |
| ML-OCC-004 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):13 | ``` This section states in advance the position this paper takes with respect to existing theories of consciousness, organized into four points. Detailed comparison is given in §18. The purpose here is to fix the coordinate frame that the reader should maintain throughout the paper. ``` |
| ML-OCC-005 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):15 | ``` \textbf{First: an opening model, not a generative model} ``` |
| ML-OCC-006 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):17 | ``` This paper does not treat consciousness as an output generated somewhere. Many models — including Global Workspace, IIT, and Higher-Order theories — describe consciousness as the product of a process or as the endpoint of an integration measure. This paper does not adopt that premise as its starting point. ``` |
| ML-OCC-007 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):19 | ``` More precisely, consciousness is not modeled here as a generated object. It is treated as a state (a log-referencing state) that opens when a specific speed difference, coupling viscosity, internal delay, and meaning gradient are established between fast predictive processes and slow sedimentary memory processes. This difference is not merely definitional; it concerns the structure of the question itself — what should be asked about consciousness. ``` |
| ML-OCC-008 | [S01 EN](../../consciousness/sections/introduction.tex):43 | ``` Note also that the two-layer structure of fast and slow layers treated in this paper is proposed in relation to IFGT (Information Field Geometry Theory), Bio-IFGT, and the cognitive-window projection developed in Conceptual Topography. IFGT supplies the general language of information density, flow, and potential; Bio-IFGT supplies life-attractor vocabulary and a finite toy hierarchy that motivate a biological comparison; CT describes how conceptual terrains stabilize within a cognitive observation window. Bio-IFGT does not contain a dedicated fast/slow confirmation result, so the two-layer interpretation developed in §4 is a premise of this paper rather than an established Bio result.  ``` |
| ML-OCC-009 | [S02 EN](../../consciousness/sections/intuitive_picture.tex):4 | ``` Intuitively, there are at least two speeds within the system. One is a fast stream handling prediction, reaction, and momentary updating. The other is a slow stream handling memory, stabilization, and long-term structural change. ``` |
| ML-OCC-010 | [S02 EN](../../consciousness/sections/intuitive_picture.tex):8 | ``` This boundary is not a static location. It is a dynamic relation that obtains only when the speed difference between the fast and slow processes is maintained while their coupling does not break. | ML-OCC-122 | [S10 EN](../../consciousness/sections/experimental_reinterpretation.tex):157 | ``` It is the natural behavior of the slow log-referencing system attempting to maintain coherence and fill in missing logs. ``` |

| ML-OCC-140 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):4 | ``` This section organizes the central thesis of this paper — consciousness is not modeled as a generated object — as an opening model. ``` |
| ML-OCC-141 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):14 | ``` \subsection{The Generation Assumption} ``` |
| ML-OCC-142 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):17 | ``` Most discussions of consciousness adopt a generation model, explicitly or implicitly. ``` |
| ML-OCC-143 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):19 | ``` In the generation model, consciousness is treated as something produced by some processing. ``` |
| ML-OCC-144 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):21 | ``` Under this view, the following questions naturally arise: which brain region generates consciousness; which circuit produces it; which computational quantity gives rise to it; how much consciousness is generated; can generated consciousness be stored or copied? ``` |
| ML-OCC-145 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):25 | ``` But they all contain the presupposition of treating consciousness as a product: a generator, a generation process, and a generated object. ``` |
| ML-OCC-146 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):27 | ``` This three-term structure is the foundation of the generation model. ``` |
| ML-OCC-147 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):31 | ``` Consciousness is not treated here as the product of processing; it is the state that opens when the relation between processing and logs enters a certain temporal condition. ``` |
| ML-OCC-148 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):33 | ``` \subsection{From Product to Relation} ``` |
| ML-OCC-149 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):36 | ``` In the generation model, consciousness is treated as a product. ``` |
| ML-OCC-150 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):44 | ``` \begin{enumerate} \item Processing is running in the fast layer. \item Referenceable logs have sedimented in the slow layer. \item There is a finite speed difference $\Delta v$ between the two. \item Coupling viscosity $\mu$ lies between complete synchrony and complete separation. \item Internal delay $\tau$ is maintained as referenceable thickness. \item Meaning gradient $\nabla\Phi$ is providing a referencing direction. \item Effective torque-like quantity $\mathcal{T}_{\Phi}$ lies within the consciousness window. \end{enumerate} ``` |
| ML-OCC-151 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):54 | ``` When these conditions are met, consciousness is not "produced." ``` |

| ML-OCC-154 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):104 | ``` Qualia are therefore not the cause that produces consciousness. ``` |
| ML-OCC-155 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):108 | ``` In the generation model, qualia invite the question "where are they produced?" ``` |
| ML-OCC-156 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):137 | ``` It is the log that was transformed while past consciousness was open. ``` |
| ML-OCC-157 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):171 | ``` The opening model naturally corresponds to VED's non-closure principle. ``` |
| ML-OCC-158 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):183 | ``` The opening model therefore does not treat consciousness as an incompletely generated product. ``` |
| ML-OCC-159 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):185 | ``` It treats consciousness as the opening that obtains when non-closure has stabilized. ``` |
| ML-OCC-160 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):187 | ``` In this sense, rather than "consciousness is not generated," more precisely: "consciousness opens through not closing completely." ``` |
| ML-OCC-161 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):196 | ``` The self is the referencing pattern that stabilizes as a result of log referencing being repeated and a certain terrain forming in the slow layer. ``` |
| ML-OCC-162 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):200 | ``` The self is therefore not the cause of consciousness. ``` |
| ML-OCC-163 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):213 | ``` \begin{itemize} \item Consciousness is not a product. \item Consciousness is the state in which referenceability has opened between fast processing and slow logs. \item The plasticity gate is not a device that produces consciousness but a boundary condition regulating log-referencing opening. \item Qualia are the live foreground appearing at the opening boundary. \item What is stored is not consciousness but the logs updated while consciousness was open. \item What can be copied is logs and structure, not consciousness itself. \item Consciousness opens within the range of temporal non-closure, not at complete closure or complete opening. \item The self is not the cause of consciousness but the slow-layer terrain formed through the repetition of opening. \end{itemize} ``` |
| ML-OCC-164 | [S12 EN](../../consciousness/sections/opening_model.tex):226 | ``` Consciousness is not modeled here as a generated object. ``` |

| ML-OCC-176 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):202 | ``` Measurement is the operation of stabilizing a state and converting it into a recordable form. ``` |
| ML-OCC-177 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):213 | ``` This section organized measurement not as external observation of the conscious state but as transformation of the opening state. ``` |
| ML-OCC-178 | [S13 EN](../../consciousness/sections/measurement_collapse.tex):215 | ``` \begin{itemize} \item Qualia are the live boundary state appearing at the sedimentation frontier. \item Measurement sediments that boundary state into the slow layer. \item What remains after measurement is sedimented residue, not qualia themselves. \item As measurement precision increases, residue becomes more definite, but live qualia are closed. \item Introspection is also internal measurement and logs qualia. \item Measurement is not error but transformation for handling boundary states. \item What should be experimentally treated is not qualia themselves but the conditions and residue of qualia sedimentation. \end{itemize} ``` |

| ML-OCC-227 | [S18 EN](../../consciousness/sections/discussion.tex):222 | ``` \paragraph{The Position of This Paper in the Four-Layer Correspondence} This transition, in which consciousness opens, the self sediments, and intelligence flows back into the world, corresponds to the role of the consciousness layer as a transitional layer within the four-layer correspondence of the VED series: physical layer, biological layer, consciousness layer, and intelligence layer \cite{Intelligence}. The consciousness window of this paper obtains on the quasi-closure basis of the biological layer and provides the intra-individual precursor form for the crossing necessity of the intelligence layer. All four layers describe the same differential-development structure of "trying to close but not closing completely" as projections within their respective observation windows. The scope of this paper is limited to the consciousness window, but there are clear connection points outside that scope. ``` |
| ML-OCC-228 | [S18 EN](../../consciousness/sections/discussion.tex):230 | ``` \begin{itemize} \item This paper treats consciousness not as a product but as the opening of log referencing. \item It has points of contact with Global Workspace, IIT, Higher-Order, and Predictive Processing discussions, but is not identical to them. \item This paper does not reduce consciousness to a single site, single quantity, or single representation. \item It does not deny neuroscience; it reinterprets neural correlates as local conditions of the log-referencing loop. \item IFGT and VED are used not as theories that fully explain consciousness but as coordinate systems for positioning it. \item The claim of this paper is shifting the question in consciousness research from "generation" to "opening conditions." \item Unresolved tasks remain, but this corresponds not only to the incompleteness of the theory but also to the non-closure of consciousness as its object. \end{itemize} ``` |

| ML-OCC-238 | [S20 EN](../../consciousness/sections/correspondence_map.tex):4 | ``` For readers who will also read Conceptual Topography (CT) alongside this paper, the correspondence between the main concepts of both papers is shown here. ``` |
| ML-OCC-239 | [S20 EN](../../consciousness/sections/correspondence_map.tex):6 | ``` This paper and CT describe different projected windows of the same structure of differential development. The variables of the two papers stand in correspondence, but they are not simply identical across layers. Neither paper grounds the other. | ML-OCC-248 | [S01 JA](../../consciousness/sections_ja/introduction_ja.tex):42 | ``` なお、本稿で扱う高速層・低速層の二層構造は、IFGT（Information Field Geometry Theory）、Bio-IFGT、および Conceptual Topography における認知的観測窓の射影との関係として提案される。IFGT は情報密度・情報流・情報ポテンシャルの一般語彙を与え、Bio-IFGT は生物学的比較を動機づける生命アトラクター語彙と有限toy hierarchyを与え、CT は概念地形が認知的観測窓の中でどのように安定化するかを記述する。Bio-IFGTには専用のfast/slow確認結果がないため、§4で展開する二層解釈はBioの確立済み結果ではなく本稿の前提である。  ``` |

### Checks performed

Source paths / current TeX inputs / figure referencesを現物照合。Phase01の全178 exact expression出現をcurrent42 sectionで順序・raw内容まで照合し、EN/JA全counterpartを保持した。役割表は全ID一意、20新uncertaintyは旧001–038と非重複、table列・fence・local links・count consistencyを検査した。

作業開始時のexisting 1,446 file SHA256 snapshotと終了時を比較し、README以外の既存file差分0を確認した。source/equation/figure/PDF/code/upstream/downstream/歴史記録はbyte不変。今回追加は本recordだけ。既存dirty working treeの差分を今回作業に計上しない。

PDF skillに従いcurrent図PDFのtextと一時renderを確認した。EN6図、JA高priority FIG-03/06のvisual inspection、EN/JA main PDFの存在とpage metadataを確認。PDF再build・simulation・web researchは実施しない。役割監査の図spot-checkであってdistribution全体のfreshness認定ではない。

一時helper・snapshot・renderはrepository外 `${TEMP_EVIDENCE_ROOT}/consciousness_phase02.6VyWKl/`。ledgerから件数を再計算し、source/local links 1,262参照・ID・table列・TeX inputs/environments・source pathsの検査errorは0。EN PDF 83 pages、JA PDF 97 pages。178 raw displayed blocksをsource順で改行結合したSHA256は `c3ef85df91bb704c3b482e2fc12574b4cf7c305a2bb6af04bbf81504b8ee6ebe`。Phase01 exact formとcurrent sourceは178/178一致し、式追加・削除・変更は0。`git diff --check`はPASS。

## 30. Stop boundary

Phase 02 COMPLETE。sourceのformal role、neural interpretation、cognitive function、phenomenal identity wording、role conflation、necessary/sufficientの差、role tensionを記録した。

導出のvalid/invalid、理論の妥当性、循環のvicious/benign、preloadの問題性、実証十分性、phenomenal explanation成否、qualia bridge可能性、claimの改訂dispositionは判定していない。記号・式・source・figure・PDFは変更しない。上流VED/Bio closureは維持し、OPEN identity/translationは解消したと扱わない。

Phase 03 preload / observer boundary / self-reference / circularity auditには着手せず停止する。
